# PRICE-42 evidence: the pricing implementation switch (cutover)

**Verified revision:** workspace `5f841dd841d0784c0166164f7d3f5bc07d8abff7` (branch `PRICE-42-cutover`), with the sibling
pricing checkout at `e51f047a768ff441329519afece0526ab366b0d5` (pricing `main` after PR #23). Only this file changed after it.

## Setup

- Machine: Windows 11, Node v24.18.0, Go 1.27.4, Java 21. Folder layout: `D:\demo-s2\workspace` (this repo) next to
  `D:\demo-s2\2026-spd-shop-pricing` (fresh `git clone`, `git checkout e51f047`). Ports 8281–8287, checked free before each run.
- Builds in the pricing checkout (each exit 0):
  - `npm ci` and `npm run build` → `dist/server.js`
  - `go -C go build -o pricing.exe .` → `go/pricing.exe`, 10016256 bytes, stamped `vcs.revision=e51f047…`, `vcs.modified=false`
  - `kotlin/gradlew -p kotlin build -x test --no-daemon` → `kotlin/build/libs/pricing.jar`, 1869436 bytes
    (unit tests ran in CI on the merged pricing PRs; not re-run here)
- Driver: a Node script outside both repos starts `node scripts/up.mjs --only pricing --pricing-port <port> [...]` from this
  repo, waits for `GET /catalog` 200, records which process listens on the port (PowerShell `Get-NetTCPConnection` →
  `Get-Process`), runs the oracle from the pricing checkout, then stops up.mjs by its PID (`taskkill /PID <pid> /T /F`)
  and checks that the service PID is gone and the port is free.

## Results

| Invocation | Process on the port | `node parity/check.mjs` | exit |
|---|---|---|---|
| no flag, no env (default) | `node` (dist/server.js) | 130/130 gating, quirks 6/6 same as TS, PASS | 0 |
| `--pricing-impl ts` | `node` (dist/server.js) | 130/130, quirks 6/6, PASS | 0 |
| `--pricing-impl go` | `pricing` (go\pricing.exe) | 130/130, quirks 0/6, PASS | 0 |
| `--pricing-impl kotlin` | `java` | 130/130, quirks 2/6, PASS | 0 |
| env `PRICING_IMPL=go` | `pricing` (go\pricing.exe) | 130/130, quirks 0/6, PASS | 0 |
| env `PRICING_IMPL=kotlin` | `java` | 130/130, quirks 2/6, PASS | 0 |

After every run the service PID was gone and the port was free. Negative paths (same revision):

```text
$ node scripts/up.mjs --only pricing --pricing-impl kotlin --pricing-port 8287      # with the jar renamed away
[pricing] kotlin/build/libs/pricing.jar not found in D:\demo-s2\2026-spd-shop-pricing. Build it first: D:\demo-s2\2026-spd-shop-pricing\kotlin\gradlew.bat -p D:\demo-s2\2026-spd-shop-pricing\kotlin build
exit code: 1
$ node scripts/up.mjs --only pricing --pricing-impl rust
PRICING_IMPL must be ts, go or kotlin (got "rust")
exit code: 64
```
